sequenceDiagram
  participant H as Android HMI
  participant A as AP VehicleService
  participant G as AP-CP Gateway
  participant C as CP Seat SWC

  C-->>G: authoritative state
  G-->>A: state + epoch + sequence
  A-->>H: Field/Event update
  Note over H,E: accepted is not Completed; UI trusts returned actual state

sequenceDiagram
    participant HMI as Android HMI
    participant MW as Vehicle Middleware / Proxy
    participant HVAC as HVAC SOME/IP Provider
    participant ECU as HVAC ECU State Machine
    participant ACT as Actuator / Sensor

    HMI->>MW: SetTargetTemperature(22, transactionId)
    MW->>HVAC: SOME/IP REQUEST: SetTargetTemperature
    HVAC->>ECU: validate + schedule command
    ECU-->>HVAC: accepted / rejected
    HVAC-->>MW: RESPONSE(E_OK / error)
    MW-->>HMI: accepted state

    HMI->>MW: subscribe(TargetTemperature eventgroup)
    MW->>HVAC: SubscribeEventgroup
    HVAC-->>MW: Subscribe ACK
    Note over HVAC,MW: ACK åªè¯´æ˜Žè®¢é˜…å…³ç³»å»ºç«‹
    ECU->>ACT: send target to actuator
    ACT-->>ECU: actual state / fault feedback
    ECU->>HVAC: authoritative field changed
    HVAC-->>MW: NOTIFICATION(TargetTemperatureChanged, epoch, seq)
    MW-->>HMI: UI updates to confirmed value
